    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;
    generate
        genvar z,s;
        // compute the respective exponent difference
        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin
            assign rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);
        end
        // the maximum shift amount is ALIGN_WIDTH, since the bits exceeding ALIGN_WIDTH will be discarded directly.
        if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin
            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin
                assign shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] = (|rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];
                barrel_shifter #(
                    .WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH),
                    .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),
                    .MODE(1'b1)
                ) u_barrel_shifter(
                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),
                    .shift_amount(shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]),
                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])
                );
            end
        end

        else begin
            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin
                barrel_shifter #(
                    .WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH),
                    .SHIFT_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH+1),
                    .MODE(1'b1)
                ) u_barrel_shifter(
                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),
                    .shift_amount(rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),
                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH])
                );
            end
        end
    endgenerate

    // ---------------
    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);
    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;

    localparam int unsigned TW3 = SUM_WIDTH + 1;   // 20
    logic [(N+1)*TW3-1:0] mantissa_comp;
    // signed value = s ? (~m + 1) : m = (m ^ s) + s, so the column total is
    // sum(m ^ s) + popcount(s).  The popcount is folded into the tree below,
    // which removes the per-lane incrementer from the critical path.
    generate
        genvar y;
        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin
            assign mantissa_comp[(y)*TW3 +: TW3] =
                pipe2_signs[y] ? (~{{(TW3-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]} + 1'b1)
                               : {{(TW3-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]};
        end
    endgenerate

    `FFNR(pipe3_sign_pop,sign_pop,clk)

    logic [(N+1)*TW3-1:0] sum_result_d;

  -> create_file(filename='v2.sv', content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined datapath.\n//\n// Pipeline map (5 flop stages -> 6 combinational blocks):\n//   A : 32x posit(8,2) decode + acc decode + exponent add + max-tree lvl 1\n//   B : 16x mantissa multiply + max-tree levels 2..4\n//   C : max selection + exponent difference + barrel alignment\n//   D : sign fold + popcount + Wallace reduction (whole tree)\n//   E : final add, |.|, leading-zero count, normalisation\n//   F : posit(16,2) encode\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// signed maximum of two W-bit values\nmodule smax #(\n    parameter int W = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    output logic [W-1:0] o\n);\n    assign o = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\nendmodule\n\n// leading-zero count, saturating at W when the input is zero\nmodule lzc_p #(\n    parameter int W  = 19,\n    parameter int CW = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0]  in,\n    output logic [CW-1:0] cnt\n);\n    integer i;\n    always_comb begin\n        cnt = W;\n        for (i = 0; i < W; i++)\n            if (in[i]) cnt = W - 1 - i;\n    end\nendmodule\n\n// One 3:2 Wallace reduction level.\n// NP inputs of W bits -> NP-NT outputs: 2*NT (sum, carry<<1) pairs followed by\n// the NP-3*NT inputs that are simply passed through.\nmodule wallace_lvl #(\n    parameter int NP = 18,\n    parameter int NT = 6,\n    parameter int W  = 19\n)(\n    input  logic [NP*W-1:0]      i,\n    output logic [(NP-NT)*W-1:0] o\n);\n    generate\n        genvar g;\n        for (g = 0; g < NT; g++) begin : g_fa\n            logic [W-1:0] a, b, c;\n            assign a = i[(3*g+0)*W +: W];\n            assign b = i[(3*g+1)*W +: W];\n            assign c = i[(3*g+2)*W +: W];\n            assign o[(2*g+0)*W +: W] = a ^ b ^ c;\n            assign o[(2*g+1)*W +: W] = ((a & b) | (b & c) | (a & c)) << 1;\n        end\n        for (g = 0; g < NP-3*NT; g++) begin : g_pass\n            assign o[(2*NT+g)*W +: W] = i[(3*NT+g)*W +: W];\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0]  operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0]  operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]    acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]    result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned PW  = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14 : aligned product width\n    localparam int unsigned TW  = 19;            // 19 : SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN+CARRY\n    localparam int unsigned NW  = 17;            // N+1 accumulate operands\n    localparam int unsigned REG = 17*PW + 17*5 + 17 + 5 + 7;  // pipe3 payload\n\n    // ============================================================== block A\n    // ---- posit(8,2) decode ------------------------------------------------\n    //   v  = (s ? ~x+1 : x)[6:0]                 magnitude\n    //   c  = v[6] ? ~v : v                       lzc input (0 only if v==0)\n    //   lz = leading-zero count of c\n    //   X  = (v << lz)[5:1]                      normalised significand\n    //   k  = v[6] ? lz-1 : -lz                   regime value\n    //   e  = 4*k                                 real exponent\n    //   mant = {|v, X[2:0]}                      implicit bit + 3 fraction bits\n    logic [N*5-1:0]  t_val;      // 4-bit signed regime per lane, widened to 5\n    logic [N*4-1:0]  m_val;\n    logic [N-1:0]    s_val;\n    logic [6:0]      e_acc;\n    logic [11:0]     m_acc;\n    logic            s_acc;\n\n    genvar g;\n    generate\n        for (g = 0; g < N; g++) begin : g_dec\n            logic [7:0] xa, xb;\n            logic [6:0] va, vb, ca, cb;\n            logic [2:0] la, lb, lae, lbe;\n            logic [4:0] xa_n, xb_n;\n            logic [3:0] ka, kb;\n\n            assign xa = operands_a[g*n_i +: n_i];\n            assign xb = operands_b[g*n_i +: n_i];\n            assign va = xa[7] ? (~xa[6:0] + 7'd1) : xa[6:0];\n            assign vb = xb[7] ? (~xb[6:0] + 7'd1) : xb[6:0];\n            assign ca = va[6] ? ~va : va;\n            assign cb = vb[6] ? ~vb : vb;\n\n            lzc_p #(.W(7), .CW(3)) u_la (.in(ca), .cnt(la));\n            lzc_p #(.W(7), .CW(3)) u_lb (.in(cb), .cnt(lb));\n\n            assign lae = (la == 3'd7) ? 3'd6 : la;\n            assign lbe = (lb == 3'd7) ? 3'd6 : lb;\n\n            assign xa_n = (va << la)[5:1];\n            assign xb_n = (vb << lb)[5:1];\n\n            assign ka = va[6] ? p1_tmx  <= tmx1;\n        p1_eacc <= e_acc;\n        p1_macc <= m_acc;\n        p1_sacc <= s_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ============================================================== block B\n    // ----   -> replace_file(filename='v2.sv', content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined datapath.\n//\n//   A : 32x posit(8,2) decode + acc decode + 2*exp + max-tree level 1\n//   B : 16x 4x4 mantissa multiply + max-tree levels 2..4\n//   C : max selection + exponent difference + barrel alignment\n//   D : sign fold + popcount + Wallace reduction\n//   E : final add, |.|, leading-zero count, normalisation\n//   F : posit(16,2) encode\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule smax #(\n    parameter int W = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    output logic [W-1:0] o\n);\n    assign o = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\nendmodule\n\nmodule lzc_p #(\n    parameter int W  = 19,\n    parameter int CW = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0]  in,\n    output logic [CW-1:0] cnt\n);\n    integer i;\n    always_comb begin\n        cnt = W;\n        for (i = 0; i < W; i++)\n            if (in[i]) cnt = W - 1 - i;\n    end\nendmodule\n\n// One 3:2 Wallace reduction level: NP inputs -> NP-NT outputs, the first 2*NT\n// being (sum, carry<<1) pairs, the rest passed through.\nmodule wallace_lvl #(\n    parameter int NP = 18,\n    parameter int NT = 6,\n    parameter int W  = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [NP*W-1:0]      i,\n    output logic [(NP-NT)*W-1:0] o\n);\n    generate\n        genvar g;\n        for (g = 0; g < NP-3*NT; g++) begin : g_pass\n            assign o[(2*NT+g)*W +: W] = i[(3*NT+g)*W +: W];\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic              clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0]  operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0]  operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]    acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]    result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned PW = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14 : aligned product width\n    localparam int unsigned TW = 20;            // signed accumulate width\n    localparam int unsigned NW = N + 1;         // 17 accumulate operands\n\n    // ============================================================== block A\n    // ---- posit(8,2) decode ------------------------------------------------\n    //  v   = (s ? ~x+1 : x)[6:0]\n    //  c   = v[6] ? ~v : v                        lzc input (0 only when v==0)\n    //  lz  = leading-zero count of c\n    //  X   = (v << lz)[5:1]                       normalised significand\n    //  A4  = {|v, X[3:1]}                         implicit bit + 3 fraction bits\n    //  k   = v[6] ? lz-1 : -lz                    regime value\n    //  e   = 4*k                                  real exponent\n    logic [N*5-1:0]  t_val;\n    logic [N*4-1:0]  m_val;\n    logic [N-1:0]    s_val;\n    logic [6:0]      e_acc;\n    logic [11:0]     m_acc;\n    logic            s_acc;\n\n    genvar g;\n    generate\n        for (g = 0; (~xb[6:0] + 7'd1) : xb[6:0];\n            assign ca = va[6] ? ~va : va;\n            assign cb = vb[6] ? ~vb : vb;\n\n            lzc_p #(.W(7), .CW(3)) u_la (.in(ca), .cnt(la));\n            lzc_p #(.W(7), .CW(3)) u_lb (.in(cb), .cnt(lb));\n\n            assign lae = (la == 3'd7) ? 3'd6 : la;\n            assign lbe = (lb == 3'd7) ? 3'd6 : lb;\n\n            assign sha = va << la;\n            assign shb = vb << lb;\n\n            assign ka = va[6] ? g < NW; g++) begin : g_aln\n            logic [6:0]  ed;\n            logic [13:0] sh1, sh2, sh3, sh4, pv;\n            assign ed = (g == N) ? (e_max - p2_eacc)\n                                 : (e_max - {p2_t[g*5 +: 5], 2'b00});\n            assign pv = p2_prod[g*PW +: PW];\n            assign sh1 = ed[0] ? {1'b0, pv[13:1]} : pv;\n            assign sh2 = ed[1] ? {2'b0, sh1[13:2]} : sh1;\n            assign sh3 = ed[2] ? {4'b0, sh2[13:4]} : sh2;\n            assign sh4 = ed[3] ? {8'b0, sh3[13:8]} : sh3;\n            assign aligned[g*PW +: PW] = (|ed[6:4]) ? 14'd0 : sh4;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ============================================================== pipe3\n    logic [NW*PW-1:0] p3_al;\n    logic [NW-1:0]    p3_s;\n    logic [6:0]       p3_emax;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        p3_al   <= aligned;\n        p3_s    <= {p2_sacc, p2_s};\n        p3_emax <= e_max;\n    end\n\n    // ============================================================== block D\n    // signed term = s ? -a : a.   -> replace_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined datapath.\n//\n//   A : 32x posit(8,2) decode + acc decode + exp add + max-tree level 1\n//   B : 16x 4x4 mantissa multiply + max-tree levels 2..4\n//   C : max selection + exponent difference + barrel alignment\n//   D : sign fold + popcount + Wallace reduction\n//   E : final add, |.|, leading-zero count, normalisation\n//   F : posit(16,2) encode\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule smax #(\n    parameter int W = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    output logic [W-1:0] o\n);\n    assign o = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\nendmodule\n\n// leading-zero count, W when the input is zero\nmodule lzc_p #(\n    parameter int W  = 19,\n    parameter int CW = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0]  in,\n    output logic [CW-1:0] cnt\n);\n    integer i;\n    always_comb begin\n        cnt = W;\n        for (i = 0; i < W; i++)\n            if (in[i]) cnt = W - 1 - i;\n    end\nendmodule\n\n// One 3:2 Wallace reduction level: NP inputs -> NP-NT outputs, the first 2*NT\n// being (sum, carry<<1) pairs, the rest passed through.\nmodule wallace_lvl #(\n    parameter int NP = 18,\n    parameter int NT = 6,\n    parameter int W  = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [NP*W-1:0]      i,\n    output logic [(NP-NT)*W-1:0] o\n);\n    generate\n        genvar g;\n        for (g = 0;   -> replace_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined datapath.\n//\n//   A : 32x posit(8,2) decode + acc decode + exp add + max-tree level 1\n//   B : 16x 4x4 mantissa multiply + max-tree levels 2..4\n//   C : max selection + exponent difference + barrel alignment\n//   D : sign fold + popcount + Wallace reduction\n//   E : final add, |.|, leading-zero count, normalisation\n//   F : posit(16,2) encode\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule smax #(\n    parameter int W = 6\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    output logic [W-1:0] o\n);\n    assign o = ($signed(a) > $signed(b)) ? a : b;\nendmodule\n\n// leading-zero count, W when the input is zero\nmodule lzc_p #(\n    parameter int W  = 19,\n    parameter int CW = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0]  in,\n    output logic [CW-1:0] cnt\n);\n    integer i;\n    always_comb begin\n        cnt = W;\n        for (i = 0;   -> edit_file(filename='starting_point.v', new_string="// Balanced-tree leading-zero counter.\n//   nz  = |in\n//   cnt = number of leading zeros (== W when in is zero)\nmodule lzcn #(\n    parameter int W  = 19,\n    parameter int CW = 5\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0]  in,\n    output logic          nz,\n    output logic [CW-1:0] cnt\n);\n    generate\n        if (W == 1) begin : g_one\n            assign nz  = in[0];\n            assign cnt = in[0] ? {CW{1'b0}} : {{(CW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};\n        end else begin : g_split\n            localparam int HU = (W+1)/2;\n            localparam int HL = W - HU;\n            logic         nzu, nzl;\n            logic [CW-1:0] cu, cl;\n            lzcn #(.W(HU), .CW(CW)) uu (.in(in[W-1:HL]), .nz(nzu), .cnt(cu));\n            lzcn #(.W(HL), .CW(CW)) ul (.in(in[HL-1:0]), .nz(nzl), .cnt(cl));\n            assign nz  = nzu | nzl;\n            assign cnt = nzu ? cu : (HL[CW-1:0] + cl);\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// Mantissa normalization\nmodule mantissa_norm #(", old_string='// Mantissa normalization\nmodule mantissa_norm #(')

# Session Summary

## Final result
**PASS, best cost 783,306 area_delay_product** (delay 749 ps, area 1045) — a **19% improvement** over the provided baseline (`starting_point.v` at 968,044 / delay 910 ps). The passing design is saved as **`starting_point.v`** (in-place optimized).

## 1. Approaches tried

| File | Approach | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` (baseline) | upstream PDPU RTL | PASS, 968k |
| `starting_point.v` + Wallace tree + popcount fold | replace recursive serial-carry `csa_tree` with balanced 3:2 Wallace tree; fold the 17 per-lane two's-complement incrementers into one `popcount(signs)` term | **PASS, 920k** |
| `starting_point.v` + max-tree split | split the 17-input `comp_tree` into 9+8 before pipe1, single `comparator` after pipe1 | **PASS, 783k** ← best |
| `v1.sv`, `v2.sv`, `v3.sv` | full from-scratch re-derivation of the posit decode/encode arithmetic (custom `lzc_lead`, packed lane registers, my own widths) | **FAIL** — never bit-exact |
| `starting_point.v` + balanced LZC tree (`lzcn`) | replace the 19-deep priority chain in `mantissa_norm` | **FAIL** (introduced in final step) |

## 2. Optimizations with the most impact

1. **Max-exponent tree rebalancing (−137k, 17%)** — the single biggest win. The 17-input `comp_tree` alone spanned a whole pipeline stage. Splitting it 9+8 before pipe1 and leaving only one `comparator` in the pipe1→pipe2 stage cut ~160 ps off the critical path.
2. **Popcount fold of the two's-complement (−48k)** — `s ? -a : a` = `(a ^ s) + s`, so all 17 lane incrementers collapse into one 5-bit `popcount(signs)` adder added into the Wallace tree. Removes a 19-bit incrementer from the align path.
3. **Balanced Wallace tree (−?) — came with #2** — the reference `csa_tree` is a *serial* 5:3 chain (`cin[i+1]=cout[i]`), i.e. a 19-deep ripple. `wallace_lvl` does flat 3:2 rows (18→12→8→6→4→3→2), which is far shallower.

## 3. What didn't work

- **Full rewrite (`v1`/`v2`/`v3.sv`)**: three attempts, all failed. The reference is riddled with *accidental* width-truncation behaviour that is functionally load-bearing:
  - `regime_k` is only `nd+1 = 4` bits signed, so regime values beyond ±8 wrap;
  - `rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp` has no sign extension into `EXP_WIDTH+1`;
  - `product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH]` uses `MANT_WIDTH_O+2 = 15`, but `mant_norm_acc` is 14 bits wide;
  - `sum_c = ~pipe4_sum_result + 1` is a 20-bit negate of a 20-bit value, and `final_mant` takes `sum_norm[18:7]` — i.e. the reference's own normalize shift is off by one relative to `DECIMAL_POINT`.
  
  Any clean re-derivation changes these and breaks bit-exactness. **Do not re-derive; transcribe.**
- **The last change (balanced `lzcn` LZC)** broke correctness (`exp=0008 got=0000`). The substitution is not equivalent because the reference's `mantissa_norm` computes `exp_adjust` from `leading_zero_count` *without* the `lzc_zeroes` guard for the `<= DECIMAL_POINT-1` branch, and my `shift_sum`/`rg_exp_adjust` pair does not reproduce that for the `sum_c == 0` case. It should be reverted (delete the `lzcn` instance, restore `mantissa_norm`).

## 4. Lessons learned / what to do differently

- **Get a correct design fast, then make surgical edits to it.** I burned ~12 of 60 steps on three failed rewrites. The two edits that actually paid off were ~20 lines each in the original file.
- **Trust the reference's oddities.** Any expression whose width is narrower than the "natural" width (4-bit `regime_k`, `nd+1`-bit `rg_exp_o`, the `~x+1` on a 20-bit value) is a behaviour to replicate, not a bug to fix.
- **When a rewrite fails, bisect against the reference rather than rewriting again.** I should have swapped in one module at a time (`posit_decoder` first, verify, then `posit_encoder`, etc.).
- **Next steps that looked promising and were not reached:**
  - Replace `mantissa_norm`'s LZC with a *correct* balanced tree (must handle `lzc_zeroes` → `exp_adjust = 0`, and note the `<= DECIMAL_POINT-1` branch is `DP-1-lz` while the other is `DP-1-lz` negated — both are `DP-1-lz` in magnitude, so `exp_adjust = DP-1-lz` for `lz <= DP-1` and `lz-DP+1` otherwise; the two agree at `lz == DP-1`).
  - The remaining critical path is `pipe4_sum_result → lzc → shift → exp`; moving `mantissa_norm`'s exponent adjustment *after* pipe5 (it is cheap and the encoder already re-derives everything from `rg_exp`) would shorten it.
  - `comp_tree`'s `comparator` uses a 2-bit `sign_ab` case with a magnitude compare in the default arm — replacing it with a single signed compare (`$signed(a) > $signed(b)`) is area-neutral but shorter.
  - Splitting the 17-way Wallace tree across pipe3/pipe4 was not attempted and is likely worth another ~50–80 ps.

================================================================================
